the brief
Use the full film pipeline: a 30-second short film styled as a video podcast, with both hosts on screen speaking their lines at microphones in a cosy studio. Maya, a Keynesian economist, argues that in a recession the government should spend to restore demand; Leo, an Austrian-school economist, argues that spending and cheap money cause the boom-and-bust and the economy should be left to correct itself. Each gets two short lines, ending on a friendly disagreement. Live-action look, 16:9, for YouTube, general audience.
1

Story

storytext model0 cr

Coffee, Yes; Stimulus, No · short

the story
In Cedar Studio, warm lamplight falls across a walnut table. Maya sits on the left behind Maya’s microphone; Leo sits on the right behind Leo’s microphone. A bookshelf and soft amber curtains fill the background. Both hosts remain visible in a wide shot. On-screen labels identify Maya as “Keynesian economist” and Leo as “Austrian-school economist.”

Maya leans toward Maya’s microphone. “In a recession, government should spend to restore demand and get people working.”

Leo raises an open hand beside Leo’s microphone. “But spending and cheap money fuel the boom that leads to the bust.”

The camera eases closer, keeping both hosts and both microphones in frame. Maya tilts Maya’s coffee mug toward Leo and smiles. “Then we disagree on stimulus, not on having another coffee.”

Leo lifts Leo’s coffee mug toward Maya’s coffee mug, smiling back. “Coffee, yes; stimulus, no—let the economy correct itself.”

CONTEXT
INT. Cedar Studio, night; scene 1 of 1, the whole of shot 1 (0–4 s): nothing moves during it.
Film: Maya and Leo sit at a walnut table in Cedar Studio, identified as a Keynesian economist and an Austrian-school economist. Maya argues for government spending in a recession, and Leo argues that spending and cheap money generate the boom that leads to a bust. Maya tilts her coffee mug toward Leo and jokes about another coffee; Leo lifts his mug toward hers, smiling, while insisting that the economy should correct itself.
Now: At night in Cedar Studio, maya and leo sit behind their respective microphones at walnut table, with both coffee mugs initially on the tabletop; warm illumination falls across the table, bookshelf and closed soft amber curtains remain behind them, and no exterior is visible.
Scene: Maya and Leo sit together behind their microphones at the studio table, with their coffee mugs within reach. Their disagreement about recession policy becomes a friendly exchange about coffee without resolving their economic differences.
Theme: Mutual warmth survives principled disagreement.
Moment: Maya and Leo regard one another as intellectual equals.
Treatment: Equal standing is established by a balanced locked wide two-shot, with Maya's identifying lower-third beneath the left host and Leo's beneath the right host for this shot only.
Tone: Cordial intellectual tension, equal conviction, and affectionate dry humour.

Wide shot. VIEW: from inside, with the subject, at eye level.

WORLD

- Leo: right third, about half the frame's height, facing toward Maya, screen left, a forearm's length behind walnut table, an arm span from Maya; seated upright; head angled toward Maya, hands both hands resting on walnut table; sceptical but relaxed, watching Maya.

PROPS
